# Cementitious-mortar fired-clay brick interior partition construction service

## 1. Scope and Applicability

This PCR describes construction of a non-loadbearing interior partition from fired-clay bricks and factory dry cementitious masonry premix, mixed with the actual supplied water at the work site. It begins with the physically accepted base/support interface and signed project design and ends with the unique finally accepted partition after the work package has completed its specified details, joint tooling, protection, setting, cleaning and actual corrections.

The design and supplier instructions for the actual project determine compatibility and required operations. The presence of a brick wall alone is insufficient to establish completion: every newly installed lintel, restraint, fixing, movement/slip/head detail and fire/acoustic closure required by that design belongs to the work package when it is its responsibility. Record which items already exist in the released interface and which are newly installed. A generic source that does not describe a particular detail does not remove that detail from the boundary.

This route excludes raw cement/sand/lime recipe mixing, lime-only mortar, brick or premix manufacture as foreground operations, structural base manufacture, loadbearing or exterior/cavity masonry, paint/plaster finishes, and service life or end-of-life beyond construction acceptance. Different products or starting conditions require a scope decision. Actual supplier/product instructions and project acceptance evidence govern the work; the cited general guidance supplies procedural context and no universal design certification or quantity factor.

## 3. Reference Flow

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| What | A completed non-loadbearing fired-clay brick interior partition construction service |
| How much | 1 m2 of net unique finally accepted single-plane wall surface; openings excluded once; both faces not counted twice |
| How well | Meets the actual signed project design and supplier compatibility for wall geometry, support/restraint/slip/head details, lintels, movement/fire/acoustic closure, joint finish and acceptance; no universal fire/acoustic rating is inferred |
| How long or cycle | One actual construction lot from released entry through final acceptance; enumerate receipt, preparation, laying, protection/setting, cleaning/correction and acceptance periods with actual cutoffs, no fixed curing days or asset life |
| reference_flow_link | accepted_partition_service |

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| reference_amount | 1 |
| reference_product_flow | Accepted interior brick partition construction service |
| reference_flow_property | Area `93a60a56-a3c8-19da-a746-0800200c9a66` |
| reference_unit_group | Units of area `93a60a57-a3c8-18da-a746-0800200c9a66` |
| reference_unit | m2 |
| required_qualifiers | site and geography; actual lot periods; wall/panel surface IDs and openings; thickness/bond/joint profile; non-loadbearing interior use; exact fired-clay SKU and moisture; factory cementitious premix grade/instructions; physical support and signed design release; actual restraint/lintel/movement/fire/acoustic detail schedule; temporary-support owner; actual protection/cure/cleaning conditions; final as-built acceptance; upstream dataset and packaging coverage |

A is the unnormalized positive area of the final accepted union. Actual corrections retain all inputs, energy, wastes and service availability in the lot numerator without adding another final wall area. Internal wet mortar uses actual kg and newly built uncured-wall handoffs use actual m2, neither fixed to the final reference amount. Existing-asset interface geometry/release/receipt remain independently measured m2 observations in own protocols and calculations, not product exchanges or production burdens. The final 1 m2 output is the calculated A/A identity for the same positive accepted A; it is not an assumed material yield.

## 4. Measurement and Unit Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Required property | Required unit | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `measure_accepted_area` | final service | Area `93a60a56-a3c8-19da-a746-0800200c9a66` | m2 | Derive A from final accepted single-plane union geometry, subtract openings and unaccepted work once and require positive A. Normalize each physical numerator once; A/A is calculated 1. |
| `measure_interface_area` | existing base and wall handoffs | Area `93a60a56-a3c8-19da-a746-0800200c9a66` | m2 | Physical interface geometry is observational. Its documents are evidence. Neither enters kg mass reconciliation, and no new structural material is inferred. |
| `measure_material_bridge` | all material/waste/usable exits | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Preserve current moisture and state. Convert count/length/area only using actual same-SKU/state mass evidence; do not add a whole assembly and its constituent totals. |
| `measure_gross_stock` | external staged materials and supplied water | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Gross draw equals opening intact plus external receipts minus closing intact over all relevant custody locations. It includes usable exits; exclude closing intact once only and keep waste/WIP/exits exclusive. |
| `measure_water_state` | water-bearing mixes/walls/wastes | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Track total and bound/free water by actual state/time. Internal handoff water is separate from final retained water. Hydration retains bound water; missing closure is not vapour. |
| `measure_energy_lineage` | electricity | Energy | kWh | Use actual meter deltas with factor, interval and topology, and only the eligible master residual after all inside-master direct assignment once; exact dimensional support remains independently required. |
| `measure_service_unit` | access and support | Duration | h | Use actual contract/service availability and ownership periods. Do not substitute labour, a presumed shift or unsupported dimensions. |
| `measure_species_medium` | direct elementary outputs | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Identify actual outgoing substance, particulate basis and receiving medium, with capture distinction. No output uptake or generic dust substitution. |

| recursive_input_rule | A same-category purchased completed partition or inherited unresolved partly built wall is not silently unfolded into this route. | upstream_dataset_requirement | Connect each actual external material/water/provider and waste route to an appropriate reviewed upstream dataset once. Internal material/feed and geometry handoffs carry existing ownership; document supplier packaging coverage and any separate residual packaging burdens. |
| disclosure | Publish exact route, entry/release and acceptance gates, actual component ownership and product instructions, period/custody/WIP boundaries, upstream dataset coverage, allocation decisions and unresolved quantity/identity/measurement gaps |

## 6. Process Inventory Structure

### Process Map

| process_id | process_name | inclusion | inclusion_condition | role | quantitative_reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `receive_setout` | Receive, survey and set out the designed partition | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | Inspect the released base and project design before recording entry. Reconcile delivery tickets, SKU/state weighing, rejection and storage inspection. Log all intact stock at central stores and point-of-use sublocations, including unissued goods for future lots, with one custody owner. Document every actual material and carried-packaging dispatch and its recipient. Design paperwork proves readiness but is not a material. Maintain a complete installed-versus-existing component schedule. Existing interface observation roles base_design_interface and physical_interface_release preserve actual geometry/state and release/receipt responsibility, not product inventory. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |
| `mix_mortar` | Prepare factory dry cementitious masonry mortar | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | At every actual batch record exact dry premix ID, weighed received internal feed, actual supplied water, mixing operation and product instructions. Weigh wet dispatch and match laying receipt. Measure discarded mortar, wash liquid, packaging and usable exits separately. Characterize solids and water before setting; record reaction state changes, not an invented mix recipe or retarder time. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |
| `lay_joint` | Lay, cut, restrain and finish brickwork joints | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | At each panel/pass match actual brick, wet mortar, designed ancillary and packaging receipts. Record physical base interface release, geometry, cutting method, tooling, restraint and closure installation, water, waste and releases. Preserve cut/offcut and rejected assembly dispositions and all remedial events. Dispatch the actual uncured wall area with its constituent/water state to acceptance; no extra material purchase is created. Existing interface observation roles physical_interface_construction_feed preserve actual geometry/state and release/receipt responsibility, not product inventory. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |
| `cure_accept` | Protect, cure, clean and accept the partition | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | Follow actual protection/cure and cleaning procedures, recording conditions, temporary-support ownership, cleaning/repair goods and all actual correction events. Match the received uncured wall state and actual water pools. Collect as-built evidence for every required design detail and signed acceptance. Survey only finally accepted unique surfaces for A; leave unresolved work in identified WIP. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |

The four responsibilities own the complete physical event ledger. Joint tooling is an explicit finishing responsibility within lay_joint followed by the actual tooled uncured-wall handoff. Cement-water setting is followed from mixing to the accepted state in cure_accept. Conditional water use, direct releases, packaging dispositions and remedial passes require occurrence evidence; unknown activation is a gap. No artificial extra node or material purchase is created by record subdivision.

### Process: Receive, survey and set out the designed partition (`receive_setout`)

Inspect the released base and project design before recording entry. Reconcile delivery tickets, SKU/state weighing, rejection and storage inspection. Log all intact stock at central stores and point-of-use sublocations, including unissued goods for future lots, with one custody owner. Document every actual material and carried-packaging dispatch and its recipient. Design paperwork proves readiness but is not a material. Maintain a complete installed-versus-existing component schedule.

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Separately supplied receipt wrapping product at receipt (`receive_packaging_wrapping`)

This card is only the independently supplied separately supplied receipt wrapping product at receipt in its actual SKU, material/formulation, physical state and supply gate. Activate only if it actually occurs and is outside complete assembly/provider coverage. Its composition, dimensions or count are qualifiers or nonadditive auxiliary measurements, never another purchase. The other objects formerly grouped with it are mandatory separately resolved requirements under atom_req_receive_setout_receive_packaging. No parent family total or numerical Range is assigned to this child. Range evidence need: collect this object's independent kg receipts, attributed opening/closing stock, usable returns, final installed/use, waste/loss and measurement uncertainty at the actual event and custody point. The same actual SKU/state/supply gate used for several functions has only one acquisition owner; merge its physical supply record once and treat the other use role as nonadditive, rather than repeating that receipt in multiple child cards.

- Selected flow: Separately supplied receipt wrapping product at receipt
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Use this child SKU/state independently measured stock-adjusted supply in kg, not the parent total: attributed opening + external receipts after tare - carried closing. Retain nonreturned waste/loss upstream; use exactly the original declared gross-with-outlets or consumed-only matched-return convention. Normalize once by the actual accepted reference denominator defined in sections 3–4 and its gate protocol. Internal handoff children use their independently measured same-SKU dispatch/receipt instead of another external acquisition.

- Normalization basis: per reference flow

Normalize once by the actual accepted reference denominator defined in sections 3–4 and its gate protocol. Internal handoff children use their independently measured same-SKU dispatch/receipt instead of another external acquisition.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`

###### Attributable electricity for receive, survey and set out the designed partition (`receive_setout_electricity`)

Measure the electricity attributable to this node, with meter serial, multiplier, interval and parent topology. Use direct deltas first. A master residual subtracts every inside-master directly assigned consumer exactly once; it never subtracts an outside-master direct load. Allocate only a positive residual over a complete positive eligible weight set. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established.

- Selected flow: Attributable electricity for receive, survey and set out the designed partition
- Flow property / unit: Energy / kWh
- Amount rule: actual_receive_setout_electricity/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_receive_setout`
- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 50
  - Unit: kWh
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 50 kWh/m2 upper screen for attributable electricity for receive, survey and set out the designed partition is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. Small tool and shared-site campaigns may vary widely with actual meter periods and idle loads; no machine rating or operating-hours factor is implied. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

###### Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service (`receive_setout_access_service_hoist`)

This is a lifecycle-complete provision service for one identified lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service with one Time/h reference. Activate only when hours are the measured compatible asset-service driver and the burden per that same hour covers full manufacture, maintenance, replacements and end-of-life across all consumers. Separate direct fuel/spares and provider-covered phases once. Obtain observed complete life or history plus sourced remaining use/replacement/termination/end-of-life, uncertainty, sensitivity and updates. A contract using another native reference is not converted into h without evidence; retain that unresolved actual requirement. Range evidence need: independent actual node hours, all-consumer lifetime h denominator and complete lifecycle coverage; no default life or hourly factor.

- Selected flow: Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service
- Flow property / unit: Duration / h
- Amount rule: Use actual attributable job/node asset-service hours divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator. The matching upstream burden is full asset lifecycle burden divided by evidenced all-consumer full-life service hours; its denominator is the same h. Do not divide the exchange quantity by full-life hours again, add an asset-equivalent Item(s) view, or use a material stock/count formula.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_receive_setout_access_service_hoist`

##### Product flows

###### Separately supplied partition-restraint product actually returned outward (`receive_unused_return_restraint`)

This card records only the actual usable outward return of separately supplied partition-restraint product actually returned outward, unchanged in SKU and state and matched to its original supply. It is not another external acquisition. Record actual usability, recipient and custody receipt. Closing stock and internal recovery are not outward returns. The original gross-with-outlets versus consumed-only convention applies exactly once; no avoided-production credit, parent total or family Range is copied. Under consumed-only, keep the physical return as a nonadditive audit and do not subtract its burden again. Range evidence need: obtain the independent measurement of this returned object in kg quantity, state and recipient evidence.

- Selected flow: Separately supplied partition-restraint product actually returned outward
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Independently measured actual usable outward quantity in kg for this matched SKU/state divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator; do not use its supply or family total as the return amount.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`

- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_receive_unused_return_restraint`

- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 300
  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 300 kg/m2 upper screen for actual lot-issued factory dry cementitious masonry mortar to mix_mortar is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The row retains its own real transfer or operating quantity; no supplier recipe, brick count factor or material identity is inferred. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

Record wet/dry basis, contamination, state and recipient. Historical removed host material is not a new upstream purchase or negative material supply. No recycling/avoided-production credit, parent total or family Range is inferred. Range evidence need: independent net kg dispatch weight/measurement and receiver evidence, including actual moisture/composition and uncertainty.

### Process: Prepare factory dry cementitious masonry mortar (`mix_mortar`)

At every actual batch record exact dry premix ID, weighed received internal feed, actual supplied water, mixing operation and product instructions. Weigh wet dispatch and match laying receipt. Measure discarded mortar, wash liquid, packaging and usable exits separately. Characterize solids and water before setting; record reaction state changes, not an invented mix recipe or retarder time.

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Factory dry cementitious masonry mortar (`dry_mortar`)

Receive the same weighed dry factory cementitious premix lot issued by receipt. Preserve its grade, formulation, bag or silo state and moisture. This is an internal feed; purchasing cement, sand or lime again would duplicate the upstream premix burden.

- Selected flow: Factory dry cementitious masonry mortar
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: actual_dry_mortar/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_mix_mortar`
- Sources: `bda-mortar-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 300
  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 300 kg/m2 upper screen for factory dry cementitious masonry mortar is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The row retains its own real transfer or operating quantity; no supplier recipe, brick count factor or material identity is inferred. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

###### Actual mortar-mixing and mixer-cleaning fresh water (`mix_water`)

Record supplied water drawn for this mixing operation, distinguishing mixing, equipment wash and any approved adjustment. Identify supply source and quality. Unused fresh-water exits remain in gross draw and are separately classified; fresh closing intact stock is removed once.

- Selected flow: Actual mortar-mixing and mixer-cleaning fresh water
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: actual_mix_water/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_mix_mortar`
- Sources: `bda-mortar-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 200
  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 200 kg/m2 upper screen for actual mortar-mixing and mixer-cleaning fresh water is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. It screens gross draw with losses and usable exits retained, not net installed material or a prescribed consumption rate. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

###### Attributable electricity for prepare factory dry cementitious masonry mortar (`mix_mortar_electricity`)

Measure the electricity attributable to this node, with meter serial, multiplier, interval and parent topology. Use direct deltas first. A master residual subtracts every inside-master directly assigned consumer exactly once; it never subtracts an outside-master direct load. Allocate only a positive residual over a complete positive eligible weight set. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established.

- Selected flow: Attributable electricity for prepare factory dry cementitious masonry mortar
- Flow property / unit: Energy / kWh
- Amount rule: actual_mix_mortar_electricity/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_mix_mortar`
- Sources: `bda-mortar-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 150
  - Unit: kWh
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`

###### Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service (`mix_mortar_access_service_hoist`)

This is a lifecycle-complete provision service for one identified lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service with one Time/h reference. Activate only when hours are the measured compatible asset-service driver and the burden per that same hour covers full manufacture, maintenance, replacements and end-of-life across all consumers. Separate direct fuel/spares and provider-covered phases once. Obtain observed complete life or history plus sourced remaining use/replacement/termination/end-of-life, uncertainty, sensitivity and updates. A contract using another native reference is not converted into h without evidence; retain that unresolved actual requirement. Range evidence need: independent actual node hours, all-consumer lifetime h denominator and complete lifecycle coverage; no default life or hourly factor.

- Selected flow: Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service
- Flow property / unit: Duration / h
- Amount rule: Use actual attributable job/node asset-service hours divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator. The matching upstream burden is full asset lifecycle burden divided by evidenced all-consumer full-life service hours; its denominator is the same h. Do not divide the exchange quantity by full-life hours again, add an asset-equivalent Item(s) view, or use a material stock/count formula.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_mix_mortar_access_service_hoist`

###### Off-spec or residual wet mortar waste (`mix_mortar_waste`)

- Amount rule: actual_mix_mortar_waste/A

  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 300 kg/m2 upper screen for off-spec or residual wet mortar waste is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The quantity still must reconcile to the matching material and water ledger. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value. Only actual wet mortar rejected or left over by the mixing operation belongs here, with current wet mass, dry-solid and water evidence. Laying/acceptance wall removals are not reassigned to mixing by calling them mortar waste. Actual rework or a custody return can affect this node only with independently evidenced real current-state transfer and reviewed compatible route/ownership; downstream removal stays with its actual producing node and is never reassigned merely by a waste name.

### Process: Lay, cut, restrain and finish brickwork joints (`lay_joint`)

At each panel/pass match actual brick, wet mortar, designed ancillary and packaging receipts. Record physical base interface release, geometry, cutting method, tooling, restraint and closure installation, water, waste and releases. Preserve cut/offcut and rejected assembly dispositions and all remedial events. Dispatch the actual uncured wall area with its constituent/water state to acceptance; no extra material purchase is created.

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Fired-clay masonry bricks (`bricks`)

Receive the fired-clay brick lot actually issued for laying, preserving non-refractory material identity, SKU, size, frog or perforation, current moisture and mass. Counts convert only with a measured same-SKU, same-state bridge. No second purchase occurs here. The nonexchange sending-role refinement selector is atom_req_receive_setout_issue_materials_lay_joint; issue_bricks is its per-material component ledger, not a second product output. Match this specific receiving input to that component by actual material/SKU/current state, quantity and physical custody event; do not use the umbrella aggregate as every component or repeat an external purchase.

- Selected flow: Fired-clay masonry bricks

###### Attributable electricity for lay, cut, restrain and finish brickwork joints (`lay_joint_electricity`)

Measure the electricity attributable to this node, with meter serial, multiplier, interval and parent topology. Use direct deltas first. A master residual subtracts every inside-master directly assigned consumer exactly once; it never subtracts an outside-master direct load. Allocate only a positive residual over a complete positive eligible weight set. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established.

- Selected flow: Attributable electricity for lay, cut, restrain and finish brickwork joints
- Flow property / unit: Energy / kWh
- Amount rule: actual_lay_joint_electricity/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_lay_joint`
- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 200
  - Unit: kWh
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 200 kWh/m2 upper screen for attributable electricity for lay, cut, restrain and finish brickwork joints is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. Small tool and shared-site campaigns may vary widely with actual meter periods and idle loads; no machine rating or operating-hours factor is implied. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

###### Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service (`lay_joint_access_service_hoist`)

This is a lifecycle-complete provision service for one identified lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service with one Time/h reference. Activate only when hours are the measured compatible asset-service driver and the burden per that same hour covers full manufacture, maintenance, replacements and end-of-life across all consumers. Separate direct fuel/spares and provider-covered phases once. Obtain observed complete life or history plus sourced remaining use/replacement/termination/end-of-life, uncertainty, sensitivity and updates. A contract using another native reference is not converted into h without evidence; retain that unresolved actual requirement. Range evidence need: independent actual node hours, all-consumer lifetime h denominator and complete lifecycle coverage; no default life or hourly factor.

- Selected flow: Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service
- Flow property / unit: Duration / h
- Amount rule: Use actual attributable job/node asset-service hours divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator. The matching upstream burden is full asset lifecycle burden divided by evidenced all-consumer full-life service hours; its denominator is the same h. Do not divide the exchange quantity by full-life hours again, add an asset-equivalent Item(s) view, or use a material stock/count formula.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_lay_joint_access_service_hoist`

###### Joint-finished uncured partition handoff (`uncured_partition`)

Dispatch the actual assembled and tooled wall state to protection and acceptance. Record unique surface/panel geometry and the associated constituent and water ledger; the wall may not yet meet acceptance. Remedial transfers remain separate actual events, not extra accepted reference area. For kg reconciliation only, retain separately measured same-panel/current-state whole-wall OR constituent mass records with transfer/acceptance event IDs and bridge evidence; use one mutually exclusive basis. Area itself is never converted by an assumed density or universal kg/m2 factor. Component water content is measured in that same state.

- Selected flow: Joint-finished uncured partition handoff
- Flow property / unit: Area / m2
- Amount rule: actual_uncured_partition/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`

- Collection protocol: `cp_lay_joint`

  - Upper: 4

  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 4 m2/m2 upper screen for joint-finished uncured partition handoff is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. Repeated interface or uncured-state handoffs may exceed final accepted geometry; the screen does not equate either area with material mass. ###### Separately supplied partition-restraint product actually returned outward (`lay_unused_return_restraint`)

This card records only the actual usable outward return of separately supplied partition-restraint product actually returned outward, unchanged in SKU and state and matched to its original supply. It is not another external acquisition. Record actual usability, recipient and custody receipt. Closing stock and internal recovery are not outward returns. The original gross-with-outlets versus consumed-only convention applies exactly once; no avoided-production credit, parent total or family Range is copied. Under consumed-only, keep the physical return as a nonadditive audit and do not subtract its burden again. Range evidence need: obtain the independent measurement of this returned object in kg quantity, state and recipient evidence.

- Selected flow: Separately supplied partition-restraint product actually returned outward
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Independently measured actual usable outward quantity in kg for this matched SKU/state divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator; do not use its supply or family total as the return amount.

- Normalization basis: per reference flow

The 200 kg/m2 upper screen for laying or cutting aqueous waste to custodian is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The quantity still must reconcile to the matching material and water ledger. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value. Only actual cutting/tooling/cleaning liquid or slurry collected at the laying operation belongs here, with current suspended solids/water composition and destination. Other-node wash liquid, solid discard and direct releases are separately owned. Actual rework or a custody return can affect this node only with independently evidenced real current-state transfer and reviewed compatible route/ownership; downstream removal stays with its actual producing node and is never reassigned merely by a waste name.

### Process: Protect, cure, clean and accept the partition (`cure_accept`)

Follow actual protection/cure and cleaning procedures, recording conditions, temporary-support ownership, cleaning/repair goods and all actual correction events. Match the received uncured wall state and actual water pools. Collect as-built evidence for every required design detail and signed acceptance. Survey only finally accepted unique surfaces for A; leave unresolved work in identified WIP.

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Uncured partition received for protection and acceptance (`cure_wall_feed`)

Receive the same uncured wall surface event and its constituent/water state from laying. Measure actual transfer geometry, without fixing it to one square metre. Its geometrical exchange is not itself an incoming kilogram in the material balance. For kg reconciliation only, retain separately measured same-panel/current-state whole-wall OR constituent mass records with transfer/acceptance event IDs and bridge evidence; use one mutually exclusive basis. Area itself is never converted by an assumed density or universal kg/m2 factor. Component water content is measured in that same state.

- Selected flow: Uncured partition received for protection and acceptance
- Flow property / unit: Area / m2
- Amount rule: actual_cure_wall_feed/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_cure_accept`
- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 4
  - Unit: m2
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 4 m2/m2 upper screen for uncured partition received for protection and acceptance is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. Repeated interface or uncured-state handoffs may exceed final accepted geometry; the screen does not equate either area with material mass. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

###### Attributable electricity for protect, cure, clean and accept the partition (`cure_accept_electricity`)

Measure the electricity attributable to this node, with meter serial, multiplier, interval and parent topology. Use direct deltas first. A master residual subtracts every inside-master directly assigned consumer exactly once; it never subtracts an outside-master direct load. Allocate only a positive residual over a complete positive eligible weight set. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established.

- Selected flow: Attributable electricity for protect, cure, clean and accept the partition
- Flow property / unit: Energy / kWh
- Amount rule: actual_cure_accept_electricity/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_cure_accept`
- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`

  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 100
  - Unit: kWh
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 100 kWh/m2 upper screen for attributable electricity for protect, cure, clean and accept the partition is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. Small tool and shared-site campaigns may vary widely with actual meter periods and idle loads; no machine rating or operating-hours factor is implied. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

###### Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service (`cure_accept_access_service_hoist`)

This is a lifecycle-complete provision service for one identified lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service with one Time/h reference. Activate only when hours are the measured compatible asset-service driver and the burden per that same hour covers full manufacture, maintenance, replacements and end-of-life across all consumers. Separate direct fuel/spares and provider-covered phases once. Obtain observed complete life or history plus sourced remaining use/replacement/termination/end-of-life, uncertainty, sensitivity and updates. A contract using another native reference is not converted into h without evidence; retain that unresolved actual requirement. Range evidence need: independent actual node hours, all-consumer lifetime h denominator and complete lifecycle coverage; no default life or hourly factor.

- Selected flow: Lifecycle-complete hourly hoisting availability service
- Flow property / unit: Duration / h
- Amount rule: Use actual attributable job/node asset-service hours divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator. The matching upstream burden is full asset lifecycle burden divided by evidenced all-consumer full-life service hours; its denominator is the same h. Do not divide the exchange quantity by full-life hours again, add an asset-equivalent Item(s) view, or use a material stock/count formula.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_cure_accept_access_service_hoist`

- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg

- Specificity: `site_specific`

- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`

  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 300
  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 300 kg/m2 upper screen for final cleaning water to custodian is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The quantity still must reconcile to the matching material and water ledger. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value. Only actual aqueous cleaning/remedial liquid collected by this protection/acceptance operation belongs here, with measured water/solids/solute fractions and wastewater custodian. Other-node wash liquid, solid waste and direct liquid/vapour releases are not counted again. Actual rework or a custody return can affect this node only with independently evidenced real current-state transfer and reviewed compatible route/ownership; downstream removal stays with its actual producing node and is never reassigned merely by a waste name.

The 300 kg/m2 upper screen for water vapour released during cure and final cleaning is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The wide provisional amount screen cannot override the measured water input and exclusive ending-pool constraints. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. ## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

The intended output is one accepted construction service. Internal interfaces, material transfers and original-unused usable exits do not create co-produced final services. Maintain separate material-resolved upstream and construction burden ledgers. Any net-use diagnostic is a reconciliation value only, with no second negative exchange or avoided-production claim:

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allocation_subdivide` | own_node_materials | Use directly measured node/material/event quantities before allocation. Internal issues and feeds transfer quantities and existing burdens; they are not new purchases, co-products or additional final services. |  |
| `allocation_usable_exits` | positive_original_unused_exits | Keep gross receipt/draw quantities including usable original-unused exits, and record those exits positively by same grade/current state and recipient. Separate the original upstream material burden attributable to the exit when supported; retain all construction/rework burdens with the lot. A net-use diagnostic may subtract exits once for reconciliation only: it is not another exchange or negative credit. No avoided-production claim is created. |  |
| `allocation_power` | four_node_electricity | Apply direct converted meter deltas first. Calculate the actual master residual after subtracting every inside-master direct assignment once, excluding outside-master direct loads from subtraction. Zero residual needs no weights. Allocate a positive residual only over all eligible consumers with a positive denominator and causal evidence; reconcile aggregate assignments to actual master/direct totals once. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |  |
| `allocation_service` | four_node_access_service | Identify every shared scaffold/hoist/extraction/protection asset, provider/service boundary and consuming node/period. Assign direct actual availability first, then only the residual of the same service interval using a complete eligible positive denominator and recorded causal basis. Actual owned idle and rework remain included. No labour proxy, presumed shift, whole asset repurchase or invented life applies. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |  |
| `allocation_period` | all_event_periods | Carry cutoff intact stock and processed WIP with their exact custody and period links. Maintain recorded attribution decisions for shared assets, replacements and termination by period; close overlaps and gaps before finalization. The same purchase, meter delta, service interval or event cannot be assigned again in the next period. |  |
| `allocation_rework` | rejected_and_corrected_states | Retain every actual correction event and link any loop to its producing and receiving nodes. Distinguish usable original goods, rework material, discarded waste and unresolved WIP. Repeated physical work remains in the numerator; only unique finally accepted geometry enters A. A downgraded/recovered destination needs actual evidence and a reviewed burden decision, not an assumed credit. | `bda-workmanship-2023` |
| atom_grouped_ledgers | all actual-child records | Apply each existing formula only to its applicable role: material stock, asset lifecycle fraction/hourly provision, completed service event, internal transfer and species/fate calculations are distinct. Never apply material-stock or full-machine purchase equations to an asset share or service event. Within the applicable method group, use concrete child identity/material, state, one exchange property/unit, receiving medium and ownership/event scope. Native and converted views are nonadditive. A family diagnostic total is never a flow identity or every child numerator; keep incomplete portions explicit. Complete formulations, assemblies and genuinely bounded single-function services remain whole, with included constituents/resources not purchased again. |  |


## 8. Foreground Data Collection, Calculation, and Quality Rules

### Data Collection Protocols

Each protocol owns exactly one real node. Typed raw arrays or scalar amounts preserve native units, actual state, uncertainty, event and period grain. Node-prefixed common fields identify physical events, electronic revisions, material/SKU/state, sublocation/custody, design/instruction references, acceptance, correction parents and dispositions. Role-prefixed fields contain measured quantities or actual conversion evidence for inventory roles, explicit observational roles and component-ledger selectors; only the declared inventory roles create exchanges. Reading/service interval arrays retain serial or asset ID, start/end, source period, native unit, conversion factor, parent boundary, consumer and assignment evidence. Consumer-weight arrays enumerate every eligible consumer and its actual causal weight.

| protocol_id | process_id | flow_role | record_type | raw_fields | collection_method | unit | frequency | temporal_coverage | site_scope | aggregation_rule | quality_evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

Log all intact stock at central stores and point-of-use sublocations, including unissued goods for future lots, with one custody owner. Document every actual material and carried-packaging dispatch and its recipient. Design paperwork proves readiness but is not a material. Maintain a complete installed-versus-existing component schedule. Every raw amount retains native unit and same-ID/current-state bridge. Read meter deltas with lineage/interval evidence and service records with actual provider/contract boundary. Record physical_event_id separately from electronic record_revision; retain actual corrections and deduplicate copies only. Pool lineage records must identify the own source card and calculation output, physical event, material/SKU, current moisture/state, period, measured same-state kg or measured portion fraction, uncertainty and exclusive destination. WIP observations link their prior cutoff or originating event. For wall states record separately measured whole OR constituent mass and bridge to the exact panel/transfer/acceptance state; Area never enters kg arithmetic. No manually entered summary pool may replace this lineage. Explicit existing-asset observation roles: base_design_interface, physical_interface_release. They retain independently measured geometry, surface/event IDs, current state and design/release/acceptance gate responsibility only, not product exchanges.  Explicit component-ledger selectors: issue_bricks inside atom_req_receive_setout_issue_materials_lay_joint, issue_restraint_components inside atom_req_receive_setout_issue_materials_lay_joint, issue_protection_material inside atom_req_receive_setout_issue_materials_cure_accept, issue_cure_cleaning_material inside atom_req_receive_setout_issue_materials_cure_accept, receive_rejected_brick inside atom_req_receive_setout_receive_material_waste, receive_nonbrick_material_waste inside atom_req_receive_setout_receive_material_waste. Group by physical event, material/SKU, state, node, period and custodian; sum actual physical quantities once. Match same-ID internal transfer pairs before aggregation. Keep unnormalized numerators; divide by the same positive final A exactly once. Compare supplier/provider dataset start/end gates and included legs/activities to actual gates. A dataset explicitly delivering through the actual handoff can cover that leg; otherwise fill every uncovered actual leg by an independently measured, mode-appropriate transport exchange or explicitly owned direct vehicle fuel/energy and asset activity. Do not assume road/diesel, distance or a leg that did not occur; unknown gate/owner/coverage is incomplete. Separate multiple actual modes/units/functions into concrete exchanges, each one property/unit, and do not add provider transport again. For each access/hoist/scaffold/extraction service, obtain the actual single-function hourly provision boundary and coverage of manufacture, maintenance/replacement, end-of-life, operation, transport and waste as applicable. Complete provider hourly data may cover these without separate asset cards. Where partial, allocate missing actual lifecycle phases by evidenced same-driver complete-life service/all-consumer ownership, and add uncovered operation/material/waste/transport activities once under their real identities. ### Calculation Rules

A is produced by calc_accepted_area. Every actual_<row_id> is an unnormalized physical numerator in the row unit; q_<row_id> divides that numerator by A once. Each static card selector is its section 6 row_id; cp_ inputs identify own-node protocols. Provisional ranges never generate inventory quantities. Scalar outputs retain the units declared by their source cards and typed pool rules; area provenance never becomes a kg operand. Helper definitions below are also included in every parsed calculation rule that uses them.

| static_symbol | Definition |
| --- | --- |
| EVENT_SUM | Use the declared inventory-role or explicitly registered component-ledger selector and that role's own protocol raw fields to sum actual physical event amounts once after exact typed conversion. The ledger selector is not another inventory card or product identity. Duplicated electronic records collapse by physical event/revision; every real repeated work/correction event is retained. A component ledger is reconciled within its conditional umbrella, never also added as a separate umbrella exchange. |
| METER_ASSIGN | Convert actual reading deltas and assign direct energy plus only eligible master residual per topology and interval, requiring complete positive weights only for a positive residual. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |
| SERVICE_ASSIGN | Assign actual contract-defined direct service availability plus only eligible same-boundary residual; reconcile every consumer and period once. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |
| UNION_AREA | Area of unique accepted single-plane surface union minus openings and excluded/unaccepted polygons once, converted using actual survey unit evidence. |
| PORTION_MASS | Select only the recorded exclusive material/event/state portion of the named unnormalized source-card kg output. Portion records declare source_card_id, source_calc_output, physical_event_id, material/SKU, current state, period, measured portion kg or fraction with method/uncertainty, and exclusive destination. Partitioned portions must reconcile to the source output; unknown shares block closure. No area, composite-plus-constituents or already normalized value enters kg arithmetic. Each individual portion is nonnegative and cannot exceed its actual physical carrier; exclusive portions of one carrier together cannot exceed that carrier. Missing values are not zero. |
| WATER_PORTION | As PORTION_MASS, but return only measured water kg in the same source event/current state, identifying bound/free form, medium and supplied-versus-process-water origin. A wet waste total is not pure water. Multiple water pools partition the same source mass without overlap; unknown content remains a gap. Water portions and their exclusive sum cannot exceed the actual physical carrier. |
| WALL_COMPONENT_MASS | Return separately measured actual same-panel/current-state whole-wall mass OR measured constituent masses from the source area card bridge records, with event/panel/lot and paired handoff or final acceptance evidence. Require the whole-or-components partition and measurement bridge; never use area as a kg addend or an assumed density/recipe factor. Source area output identifies geometry only. |
| WIP_MASS | Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Records link prior closing stock or current retained event and distinguish intact unissued stock already removed in gross draw. Wall WIP requires the same measured whole-or-components kg bridge. Unknown quantities/ownership block closure. |
| WIP_WATER | Select measured water content of the exact processed WIP records used by WIP_MASS, by source event/current state/time, with bound/free and uncertainty. Do not include intact fresh closing stock, handoff, accepted retention or other exit pools again. |
| EVIDENCED_ZERO | Return zero only when the named own-node absence records demonstrate that no such actual pool exists for the full lot/period. Unknown, missing or nonzero records block closure and require the actual role and path to be reviewed, never conceal it in a balancing amount. |
| OBSERVED_GEOMETRY | Read the explicitly declared observational role and its own protocol raw geometry/native-unit/current-state records, preserving actual panel/surface IDs, independently measured m2, release/acceptance evidence and same-event sender/recipient responsibility. Match physical_interface_release to physical_interface_construction_feed by their real interface event and geometry/state. Unknown geometry or release status remains a blocking gate gap; documented nonoccurrence is not a presumed one. These are existing-asset observations only: no product exchange, supplier purchase, production burden, inventory Range or kg contribution is created. |
| EXCHANGE_COMPONENT_MASS | For one actual instantiated exchange identified by the recorded exchange key, select and sum only its measured events from the original component ledgers. The key includes actual material/SKU/composition, current state, physical_event_id or a documented same-SKU/state/concrete-identity aggregation of deduplicated physical events over the lot, concrete independently verified UUID when resolved, and actual custody/recipient; waste also includes phase, custodian and treatment destination. Reconcile selected event portions to their matching source component outputs, with nonnegative portions not exceeding the physical source carrier and no overlap. Return that one concrete exchange numerator in Mass/kg, never the mixed role total. Only actual kg component operands are eligible; length, area, count and existing-interface observations cannot be summed here. Any native-unit conversion must already have the measured same-SKU/current-state mass bridge. Expand the actual schedule into zero, one or many exchanges without inventing identity or quantity. Unknown key, component amount or custody/destination remains a gap. A separately named diagnostic role sum is for reconciliation only and is not another exchange. |

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `calc_accepted_area` | cure_accept | A = UNION_AREA(cure_accept_accepted_surface_polygons, cure_accept_opening_polygons, cure_accept_excluded_surface_polygons, cure_accept_geometry_unit_factor) after cure_accept_final_acceptance_status and cure_accept_gate_component_completeness_ref confirm the gate. Whole assembly OR constituents; intact unissued stock already removed at gross draw is excluded. Closing WIP is after all actual handoffs, accepted retention and exits.; receive_setout_closing_processed_wip_kg = WIP_MASS(receive_setout_closing_processed_wip_records). Every observation names a listed own card and unnormalized source output/event, exact current-state measured kg and period/custody, linking prior closing WIP or current retention. Whole assembly OR constituents; intact unissued stock already removed at gross draw is excluded. Full unnormalized own-card outputs, each counted once by actual material/event/state/period; whole recorded amounts.; receive_setout_material_internal_in_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(receive_setout_material_internal_in_kg_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown material_internal_in_kg blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review.; receive_setout_final_retained_mass_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(receive_setout_final_retained_mass_kg_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown final_retained_mass_kg blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review.; receive_setout_next_material_handoff_kg = actual_issue_dry_mortar + actual_issue_bricks + actual_issue_restraint_components + actual_issue_protection_material + actual_issue_cure_cleaning_material + actual_child_quantity[atom_req_receive_setout_issue_packaging_mix_mortar][actual_child_row_id] + actual_child_quantity[atom_req_receive_setout_issue_packaging_lay_joint][actual_child_row_id] + actual_child_quantity[atom_req_receive_setout_issue_packaging_cure_accept][actual_child_row_id]. Full unnormalized own-card outputs, each counted once by actual material/event/state/period; whole recorded amounts.; receive_setout_material_discard_kg = actual_receive_rejected_brick + actual_receive_nonbrick_material_waste + actual_child_quantity[atom_req_receive_setout_receive_packaging_waste][actual_child_row_id]. Full unnormalized own-card outputs, each counted once by actual material/event/state/period; whole recorded amounts.; receive_setout_aqueous_waste_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(receive_setout_aqueous_waste_kg_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown aqueous_waste_kg blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review.; receive_setout_direct_release_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(receive_setout_direct_release_kg_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown direct_release_kg blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review.; receive_setout_usable_exit_kg = actual_child_quantity[atom_req_receive_setout_receive_unused_return][actual_child_row_id]. Full unnormalized own-card outputs, each counted once by actual material/event/state/period; whole recorded amounts.; receive_setout_other_exclusive_transfer_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(receive_setout_other_exclusive_transfer_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown other_exclusive_transfer blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review. All pool assignments preserve the same source event/material/state/period and reconcile to actual source outputs. A source whole amount and its components use one basis only. Internal mass uses source handoff records; geometry is excluded. Original-unused positive exits remain in gross inputs and have no negative credit. Actual gross product mass excludes separately counted packaging using a measured constituent split; never add a packaged whole and its packaging again. Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown aqueous_waste_kg blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review. This absence concerns only an additional aqueous pool after excluding every liquid or other phase already counted in atom_req_receive_setout_receive_material_waste; it is not a claim that receipt can have no liquid discard. Any additional actual aqueous path needs an explicit own-node role before closure. WIP_MASS definition: Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Records link prior closing stock or current retained event and distinguish intact unissued stock already removed in gross draw. Wall WIP requires the same measured whole-or-components kg bridge. Unknown quantities/ownership block closure. EVIDENCED_ZERO definition: Return zero only when the named own-node absence records demonstrate that no such actual pool exists for the full lot/period. Unknown, missing or nonzero records block closure and require the actual role and path to be reviewed, never conceal it in a balancing amount. Component outputs actual_issue_bricks, actual_issue_restraint_components, actual_issue_protection_material, actual_issue_cure_cleaning_material belong to their declared current umbrellas; use those original per-SKU/state amounts once, never the umbrella aggregate plus components. They are ledger quantities, not phantom inventory cards. Component outputs actual_issue_bricks, actual_issue_restraint_components, actual_issue_protection_material, actual_issue_cure_cleaning_material belong to their declared current umbrellas; use those original per-SKU/state amounts once, never the umbrella aggregate plus components. All pool assignments preserve the same source event/material/state/period and reconcile to actual source outputs. A source whole amount and its components use one basis only. Internal mass uses source handoff records; geometry is excluded. Original-unused positive exits remain in gross inputs and have no negative credit. Actual gross product mass excludes separately counted packaging using a measured constituent split; never add a packaged whole and its packaging again. WIP_MASS definition: Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Unknown quantities/ownership block closure. EVIDENCED_ZERO definition: Return zero only when the named own-node absence records demonstrate that no such actual pool exists for the full lot/period. Select ONLY original unused fresh supplied-water portion linked to mix_water; no changed process liquid, other material inherent water, waste or WIP. This positive portion is already within the own usable-exit total and is not a second material exchange. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; mix_water_vapour_internal_received_water_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(mix_mortar_internal_received_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown internal_received blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review.; mix_water_vapour_material_bound_and_free_entry_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(actual_dry_mortar, mix_mortar_material_entry_water_portion_records) + WATER_PORTION(actual_child_quantity[atom_req_mix_mortar_mix_mortar_packaging_feed][actual_child_row_id], mix_mortar_material_entry_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only inherent/attached water in these otherwise received materials, including actual premix moisture or formulated solution water. Excludes fresh supplied water and the wet process handoff; factory water already in a product is not another fresh-water purchase. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; mix_water_vapour_aqueous_waste_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(actual_mix_wastewater, mix_mortar_aqueous_waste_water_kg_water_portion_records). Only actual water portion of collected wastewater, excluding suspended solids and other solutes; no duplicate direct release. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; mix_water_vapour_direct_liquid_release_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(actual_child_quantity[atom_req_mix_mortar_mix_direct_release][actual_child_row_id], mix_mortar_direct_liquid_release_water_kg_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only actually released outgoing liquid H2O component in an evidenced receiving medium, exclusive of vapour, other substances and collected wastewater. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; mix_water_vapour_other_exclusive_water_exit_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(mix_mortar_other_exclusive_water_exit_absence_records). All water entries and endings are exclusive by actual source material/event/state/time and measured portion lineage. Fresh gross equals the own supplied-water card numerator, with intact fresh closing stock already excluded once. No fresh stock subtraction appears again. WATER_PORTION definition: As PORTION_MASS, but return only measured water kg in the same source event/current state, identifying bound/free form, medium and supplied-versus-process-water origin. A wet waste total is not pure water. Multiple water pools partition the same source mass without overlap; unknown content remains a gap. Water portions and their exclusive sum cannot exceed the actual physical carrier. WIP_WATER definition: Select measured water content of the exact processed WIP records used by WIP_MASS, by source event/current state/time, with bound/free and uncertainty. Do not include intact fresh closing stock, handoff, accepted retention or other exit pools again. EVIDENCED_ZERO definition: Return zero only when the named own-node absence records demonstrate that no such actual pool exists for the full lot/period. Unknown, missing or nonzero records block closure and require the actual role and path to be reviewed, never conceal it in a balancing amount. PORTION_MASS definition: Select only the recorded exclusive material/event/state portion of the named unnormalized source-card kg output. Portion records declare source_card_id, source_calc_output, physical_event_id, material/SKU, current state, period, measured portion kg or fraction with method/uncertainty, and exclusive destination. Partitioned portions must reconcile to the source output; unknown shares block closure. No area, composite-plus-constituents or already normalized value enters kg arithmetic. Each individual portion is nonnegative and cannot exceed its actual physical carrier; exclusive portions of one carrier together cannot exceed that carrier. Missing values are not zero. WIP_MASS definition: Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Records link prior closing stock or current retained event and distinguish intact unissued stock already removed in gross draw. Wall WIP requires the same measured whole-or-components kg bridge. Unknown quantities/ownership block closure. | `actual_mix_water`; `actual_child_quantity[atom_req_mix_mortar_mix_unused_return][actual_child_row_id]`; `mix_mortar_unused_fresh_exit_water_portion_records`; `WATER_PORTION`; `mix_mortar_internal_received_absence_records`; `EVIDENCED_ZERO`; `actual_dry_mortar`; `actual_child_quantity[atom_req_mix_mortar_mix_mortar_packaging_feed][actual_child_row_id]`; `mix_mortar_material_entry_water_portion_records`; `mix_mortar_opening_processed_wip_records`; `mix_mortar_opening_processed_wip_water_records`; `WIP_WATER`; `mix_mortar_closing_processed_wip_records`; `mix_mortar_closing_processed_wip_water_records`; `actual_wet_mortar_feed`; `mix_mortar_next_handoff_water_kg_water_portion_records`; `mix_mortar_final_retained_bound_water_kg_absence_records`; `mix_mortar_final_retained_free_water_kg_absence_records`; `actual_mix_mortar_waste`; `actual_child_quantity[atom_req_mix_mortar_mix_packaging_waste][actual_child_row_id]`; `mix_mortar_solid_waste_water_kg_water_portion_records`; `actual_mix_wastewater`; `mix_mortar_aqueous_waste_water_kg_water_portion_records`; `actual_child_quantity[atom_req_mix_mortar_mix_direct_release][actual_child_row_id]`; `mix_mortar_direct_liquid_release_water_kg_water_portion_records`; `mix_mortar_other_exclusive_water_exit_absence_records`; `cp_mix_mortar`; `PORTION_MASS`; `WIP_MASS` | `mix_water_vapour_gross_fresh_draw_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_unused_fresh_exit_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_internal_received_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_material_bound_and_free_entry_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_opening_processed_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_closing_processed_wip_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_next_handoff_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_final_retained_bound_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_final_retained_free_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_solid_waste_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_aqueous_waste_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_direct_liquid_release_water_kg`; `mix_water_vapour_other_exclusive_water_exit_kg` |  |

`cure_water_vapour_final_retained_bound_water_kg`; `cure_water_vapour_final_retained_free_water_kg`; `cure_water_vapour_solid_waste_water_kg`; `cure_water_vapour_aqueous_waste_water_kg`; `cure_water_vapour_direct_liquid_release_water_kg`; `cure_water_vapour_other_exclusive_water_exit_kg` |  |

For each material/SKU/state, reconcile gross draw against actual issues, own receipt discards, positive usable exits and exclusive processed retention/WIP/other transfers. Pool totals must derive from exact own-card unnormalized outputs or measured own-state observations with physical-event/portion lineage; manually entered summaries cannot replace that lineage. Match actual custody-event issue/feed pairs. Unissued future-lot intact goods stay in receipt stock at every sublocation. Supplier packaging already covered upstream receives no second manufacturing burden, while its physical custody/waste remains visible. For each water-bearing node, process-handoff water and separately characterized entry-material moisture are exclusive origins. Ending pools separate handoff, final bound/free retention, material/wastewater fractions, actual liquid releases, original-unused fresh exits, WIP, other evidenced exits and validated vapour. An intermediate handoff is not also final retention. Gross fresh draw already excludes intact fresh closing stock once. Bound water remains retained; actual evaporation requires independent evidence and unknown gaps never become releases.

### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| atom_actual_child_selectors | all actual-child and legacy role records | Actual-child selector contract: each former collection role below is a nonexchange roster selector, never a material card or a scalar parent quantity. Legacy raw fields with that role prefix are indexed by actual_child_row_id, SKU/material, current state/moisture, one property/unit, event/period and custody. For every former calc_<role>, preserve its original stock/actual dispatch/receipt, own-node discard, current-state bridge and boundary ownership algorithm, but evaluate it separately for each applicable actual child. Its actual-role output means actual_child_quantity[requirement_id][actual_child_row_id]: the child's independently measured unnormalized quantity in ROW_UNIT(actual_child_row_id), not a requirement quantity. Mass/kg children return kg, Duration/h service children return h, and Energy/kWh children return kWh; these different units are never added together. ROW_UNIT always reads the actual child card; a requirement ID and legacy ledger alias have no exchange unit. Source_card_id/source_calc_output/portion/WIP/stock/fate/return records must reference that actual child and its same-state event, never the removed parent. PORTION_MASS and WATER_PORTION select and partition each measured physical child carrier once; the same carrier is not counted whole and by constituents. WALL_COMPONENT_MASS still uses separately measured same-panel/current-state whole OR constituents, never area-to-kg assumptions. Each role selector enumerates a finite actual child set, not a second aggregate exchange. Existing mass/water pool and closure sums mean first select the compatible actual child/material+kg/state/event records, exclude duplicate carrier/component views, and only then sum measured kg as physical QA. No heterogeneous units, normalized and raw amounts, or unresolved residuals enter a sum. A role QA sum does not become an inventory flow or every child numerator. Preserve evidenced-zero/absence, bound/free water, rework ownership, all-period stock/WIP, uncertainty and unknown-is-incomplete duties; no former numerical family Range is inherited. Nonexchange role map: receive_packaging -> atom_req_receive_setout_receive_packaging -> [receive_packaging_wrapping, receive_packaging_bag, receive_packaging_liner, receive_packaging_pallet]; receive_setout_access_service -> atom_req_receive_setout_receive_setout_access_service -> [receive_setout_access_service_hoist, receive_setout_access_service_scaffold, receive_setout_access_service_extraction]; issue_restraint_components: issue_materials_lay_joint_fixing -> restraint_components_fixing; issue_restraint_components: issue_materials_lay_joint_lintel -> restraint_components_lintel; issue_restraint_components: issue_materials_lay_joint_reinforcement -> restraint_components_reinforcement; issue_restraint_components: issue_materials_lay_joint_movement_closure -> restraint_components_movement_closure; issue_restraint_components: issue_materials_lay_joint_fire_closure -> restraint_components_fire_closure;
