issue_protection_material: issue_materials_cure_accept_cover -> protection_material_cover; issue_cure_cleaning_material: issue_materials_cure_accept_cleaner -> cure_cleaning_material_cleaner; issue_packaging_mix_mortar: issue_packaging_mix_mortar_wrapping -> mix_mortar_packaging_feed_wrapping; issue_packaging_mix_mortar: issue_packaging_mix_mortar_bag -> mix_mortar_packaging_feed_bag; issue_packaging_mix_mortar: issue_packaging_mix_mortar_liner -> mix_mortar_packaging_feed_liner; issue_packaging_mix_mortar: issue_packaging_mix_mortar_pallet -> mix_mortar_packaging_feed_pallet; issue_packaging_lay_joint: issue_packaging_lay_joint_wrapping -> lay_joint_packaging_feed_wrapping; issue_packaging_lay_joint: issue_packaging_lay_joint_bag -> lay_joint_packaging_feed_bag; issue_packaging_lay_joint: issue_packaging_lay_joint_liner -> lay_joint_packaging_feed_liner; issue_packaging_lay_joint: issue_packaging_lay_joint_pallet -> lay_joint_packaging_feed_pallet; issue_packaging_cure_accept: issue_packaging_cure_accept_wrapping -> cure_accept_packaging_feed_wrapping; issue_packaging_cure_accept: issue_packaging_cure_accept_bag -> cure_accept_packaging_feed_bag; issue_packaging_cure_accept: issue_packaging_cure_accept_liner -> cure_accept_packaging_feed_liner; issue_packaging_cure_accept: issue_packaging_cure_accept_pallet -> cure_accept_packaging_feed_pallet. For each pair require same actual SKU/state/kg/transfer_id, separately measured dispatch and receipt and original positive accepted area A; external receipt alone bears acquisition. A dispatch discrepancy cannot become a presumed loss, a receiving purchase or accepted geometry.   Definition: actual_child_quantity[requirement_id][actual_child_row_id] reads exactly that existing actual child card's independently measured unnormalized quantity in its declared ROW_UNIT(actual_child_row_id), using the role-to-child map; it is never a lookup of a requirement as an exchange. For scalar phase QA pools only, ROLE_MASS_KG[requirement_id] sums the complete finite disjoint measured kg children of that role after identity/state/event/owner checks; it has no inventory unit/reference/Range and is not another flow. WATER_PORTION applied to this QA selector must expand its per-child carriers and measured source-output portions before summing water kg; no aggregate moisture fraction or lost carrier lineage is allowed. EVENT_SUM(actual_child_row_id, protocol) selects only that real child's original-prefix raw records under the requirement; legacy aliases issue_bricks / issue_restraint_components / issue_protection_material / issue_cure_cleaning_material / receive_rejected_brick / receive_nonbrick_material_waste index those same actual children and are not extra physical rows. Every q_<former_role>[actual_child_row_id] is the one child numerator/A. Cardinalities and unknown residuals must be collected; a finite sum cannot omit unresolved actual objects as zero.| cp_receive_setout; cp_mix_mortar; cp_lay_joint; cp_cure_accept; actual child/role register; original source-output/raw-field/custody/stock/WIP/fate and water-portion evidence |
| `dq_design` | entry and final acceptance | Retain signed design, exact supplier compatibility and current project instructions for every required component and the as-built acceptance schedule. | design_release_ref; component_schedule_ref; supplier_instruction_ref; acceptance_ref |
| `dq_stock` | all material and packaging ledgers | Document every material/state/custody sublocation and exclusive final fate, including point-of-use future stock; join each gross draw, issue, feed, exit and WIP quantity to actual events. | stock_custody_partition_ref; custody evidence; scale records; upstream_coverage_ref |
| `dq_water` | each water-bearing node | Distinguish the water content of incoming/handed-over/retained/waste states at their actual time. Bound water remains mass; unexplained closure is uncertainty. | pool_partition_ref; moisture methods; evaporation_method_ref; uncertainty_kg |
| `dq_meter` | each energy or shared-service node | Keep actual serial, factors, source intervals, topology and the full eligible consumer set. Reconcile direct and residual assignments; prove the zero-residual case. | reading interval arrays; conversion_factor_ref; meter_topology_ref; contract_unit_ref |
| `dq_event` | all real occurrences | Deduplicate electronic revisions using physical event lineage, while preserving real repeated work and its node/period. Unresolved replacement/termination remains a gap. | physical_event_id; record_revision; rework_parent_event_id; period_attribution_ref |
| `dq_quantity` | all published amounts | Keep original records and bridge uncertainty, with unnormalized quantities and one A normalization. Replace provisional screens only using reviewed independent evidence; never validate a sample by its own maximum. | calibration; native quantities; actual same-state bridge; calculation audit |
| `dq_identity` | all resolved and unresolved exchanges | Resolve actual material/provider/species/medium and independent dimensional supports before claiming final downstream exchange readiness. A usable quantity does not prove database identity. | actual supplier/material and waste destination evidence; verified identity/support decisions |
| atom_req_receive_setout_receive_packaging | receive_setout | Mandatory actual-child refinement for receive_setout/inputs/product. Enumerate the distinct actual products, single-function complete services/assets, physical waste carriers or substance/medium releases covered by this role. Preserve these original applicability and evidence obligations: Account for actual wrapping, bags, liners, pallets and handling protection at receipt by composition and custody. This includes packaging still attached to materials issued later. Record whether its manufacture is already covered by each supplier dataset before adding any separate production burden.  Child-specific range evidence and uncertainty must be collected; former replaced-family numerical screens are recorded only in the migration audit and are not active child or diagnostic bounds. Actual absence needs evidence, unknown is not zero and measured valid values are never clipped. Original quantity method, applied separately to each compatible child/material+property+unit/state ledger: actual_child_quantity[atom_req_receive_setout_receive_packaging][actual_child_row_id]/A Keep independently supported named child cards; the remainder is not silently absent. Each actual child has one explicit exchange property/unit and independently collected quantity with exact identity/support before final foreground completion. Do not guess a SKU, species, medium, ratio or unit; no heterogeneous total, copied parent quantity/Range or duplicate whole-carrier/component flow. cp_atom_req_receive_setout_receive_packaging is required; missing roster/identity/unit/quantity/scope/closure keeps the foreground package incomplete, while candidate methodology may be revised without site data. | Original node protocol cp_receive_setout; independent actual-child roster and evidence |
| atom_req_receive_setout_receive_setout_access_service | receive_setout | Mandatory actual-child refinement for receive_setout/inputs/product. | atom_req_all_nodes_actual_transport_and_service_coverage | all processes | Execute cp_atom_req_all_nodes_actual_transport_and_service_coverage across receive_setout, mix_mortar, lay_joint and cure_accept. For every actual material, equipment/service, reusable return and waste movement, record actual dispatch and delivery gates, origin/destination, transfer/leg IDs, mode, payload and unit, loaded and attributable empty distance, dates, consuming node and single burden owner. Compare supplier/provider dataset start/end gates and included legs/activities to actual gates. A dataset explicitly delivering through the actual handoff can cover that leg; otherwise fill every uncovered actual leg by an independently measured, mode-appropriate transport exchange or explicitly owned direct vehicle fuel/energy and asset activity. Do not assume road/diesel, distance or a leg that did not occur; unknown gate/owner/coverage is incomplete. Separate multiple actual modes/units/functions into concrete exchanges, each one property/unit, and do not add provider transport again. For each access/hoist/scaffold/extraction service, obtain the actual single-function hourly provision boundary and coverage of manufacture, maintenance/replacement, end-of-life, operation, transport and waste as applicable. Complete provider hourly data may cover these without separate asset cards. Where partial, allocate missing actual lifecycle phases by evidenced same-driver complete-life service/all-consumer ownership, and add uncovered operation/material/waste/transport activities once under their real identities. Preserve original direct/master eligible-consumer residual allocation, actual idle/remedial periods and no labour/shift proxy. Missing phase/leg evidence remains incomplete; no automatic new fuel or asset assumption. | cp_atom_req_all_nodes_actual_transport_and_service_coverage |



## 9. Validation Rules

Validation evaluates declared route, ownership and evidence, preserving performed/skipped checks and uncertainty. Missing identities or quantities stay disclosed in their actual roles, never become zero or generic substitutes. A mathematical or structural check cannot alone certify physical design, external flow identities or publication readiness:

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `validation_gate` | scope_and_final_output | Confirm released entry design and final as-built completeness for all actual compatible restraints, lintels, movement/fire/acoustic closures and protection/cure/cleaning. Exclude unresolved panels from accepted A and retain their WIP/correction evidence. | `bda-workmanship-2023`; `ancon-restraint-interface` |
| `validation_geometry` | area_and_mass | Require A > 0 from the union of finally accepted single-plane surfaces with openings excluded once. Neither two faces, repeated acceptance forms nor rework passes multiply A. Physical interface area is observational and cannot enter a kg balance or imply newly supplied structure. |  |
| `validation_material` | gross_stock_and_transfers | Reconcile each material/SKU/state ledger from opening and external receipts through all-location intact closing stock, actual issues, own receipt waste/usable exits and exclusive WIP. Pair issue/feed by event, quantity, state and custody. All unissued future-lot stock remains in receipt custody wherever stored; no repeated purchase or second stock subtraction. |  |
| `validation_units` | conversion_and_measured_absence | Require exact same-SKU/current-state count/length/area-to-kg bridges, calibrated units and explicit composition/whole-versus-component ownership. Never sum mixed dimensions. Prove nonoccurrence before zero; missing records and conditional activation uncertainty remain gaps. |  |
| `validation_water` | mix_lay_cure | Check own-node time/state-exclusive water pools against actual gross supplied water and attached incoming water. Remove fresh closing intact once only. Separate actual handoff, final bound/free retention, wastes, liquid releases, usable exits and processed WIP. H2O to air unspecified needs actual validated evaporation; a residual or hydration state change is insufficient. | `bda-mortar-2023` |
| `validation_release` | elementary_output_roles | Require actual outgoing substance/particle basis and receiving medium for direct releases; prevent captured-dust/waste duplication. Output release cards cannot represent uptake. Any evidenced uptake needs its own reviewed input semantics; no default carbonation or universal dust identity. |  |
| `validation_meter` | meter_topology_and_service | Audit meter reading serial, source interval, factors, parent topology and complete eligible consumer set; check residual arithmetic, inside/outside boundary, zero-residual exception and aggregate reconciliation. Service h needs actual provider/contract unit and period, never an assumed shift or labour proxy. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |  |
| `validation_rework` | all_reject_routes | Each rejection has an actual state, owner and path: rework, documented usable exit, recovery/downgrade with reviewed burden treatment, waste or retained WIP. Match loop events and preserve all actual corrections. An unresolved rejected surface does not become final accepted output. | `bda-workmanship-2023` |
| `validation_periods` | period_asset_event_linkage | Enumerate all lot phases and periods and link inputs, assets, outputs and correction/replacement/termination events. Review period-specific attribution and prevent duplicate carryover of burdens or accepted geometry. Unresolved replacement or termination ownership blocks finalization. |  |
| `validation_quality` | ranges_and_identity | Treat all provisional reasoned_estimate ranges as review screens only. They are not empirical factors, acceptance limits or permission to use a missing value. Verify actual exchange identity and property/unit separately from quantity evidence; unresolved provider/material/species and Duration/Energy support remain explicit review gaps. |  |

| atom_req_all_nodes_actual_transport_and_service_coverage_complete | all processes | Execute cp_atom_req_all_nodes_actual_transport_and_service_coverage across receive_setout, mix_mortar, lay_joint and cure_accept. For every actual material, equipment/service, reusable return and waste movement, record actual dispatch and delivery gates, origin/destination, transfer/leg IDs, mode, payload and unit, loaded and attributable empty distance, dates, consuming node and single burden owner. Compare supplier/provider dataset start/end gates and included legs/activities to actual gates. A dataset explicitly delivering through the actual handoff can cover that leg; otherwise fill every uncovered actual leg by an independently measured, mode-appropriate transport exchange or explicitly owned direct vehicle fuel/energy and asset activity. Do not assume road/diesel, distance or a leg that did not occur; unknown gate/owner/coverage is incomplete. Separate multiple actual modes/units/functions into concrete exchanges, each one property/unit, and do not add provider transport again. For each access/hoist/scaffold/extraction service, obtain the actual single-function hourly provision boundary and coverage of manufacture, maintenance/replacement, end-of-life, operation, transport and waste as applicable. Complete provider hourly data may cover these without separate asset cards. Where partial, allocate missing actual lifecycle phases by evidenced same-driver complete-life service/all-consumer ownership, and add uncovered operation/material/waste/transport activities once under their real identities. Preserve original direct/master eligible-consumer residual allocation, actual idle/remedial periods and no labour/shift proxy. Missing phase/leg evidence remains incomplete; no automatic new fuel or asset assumption. |  |



## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | Foreground construction-lot data package, with downstream process or lifecyclemodel projections |
| downstream_use | secondary_dataset and/or background_dataset only within the declared accepted interior partition route and qualifiers |
| allowed_use | Construction-stage modelling of the exact defined service, using actual upstream materials, providers and destinations with reviewed scope and measurements |
| excluded_use | Universal wall design/certification; material manufacturing inventory; exterior/loadbearing/cavity/lime-only/site-recipe routes; decorative finish or building use/end-of-life inventory; assumed lifetime/curing/weather/recipe defaults |
| required_metadata | PCR identity/version; geography/site/lot and actual phase/period boundaries; brick/premix/ancillary IDs and state; component ownership/design/instruction/asbuilt acceptance; unique area and opening geometry; all stock/custody/WIP and rework event lineage; upstream dataset/packaging coverage; energy/service consumer topology and allocation; measured uncertainty |
| required_quality_disclosure | Measured/calculated quantities versus provisional screens; performed/skipped checks; identity/property/unit gaps; source age/scope limits; material/water closure and missing records; residual allocation evidence; reject/rework dispositions and unresolved period/ownership decisions |
| update_trigger | Actual product/route/design ownership, supplier instructions, accepted gate or upstream dataset boundary changes; materially revised measurements/period attribution; new supported identities or independent quantitative evidence |

## 11. Data Sources

The BDA manuals describe general clay-brick workmanship and mortar practice, including historical examples and multiple routes. They do not establish factory-premix consumption, brick dimensions, laying height, cure duration, a wetting instruction or current project weather thresholds. Mortar volume percentages, tonnes per brick counts, component ratios and retarder examples are not factors for this PCR. The restraint article explains an engineering interface; its blockwork examples do not certify every fired-clay brick. Actual project design and product instructions remain required foreground evidence.

| source_id | type | reference | used_for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| bda-workmanship-2023 | handbook | Brick Development Association, Good Site Practice and Workmanship, April 2023, 19 pages. https://d23z90m210izbx.cloudfront.net/uploads/downloads/13.-Good-Site-Practice-Workmanship-General-Guide-2023.f1678702110.pdf | pp5-8 design, reference acceptance, delivery/storage/handling; pp9-11 setout, full joints, cutting and timing/tooling; pp14-15 actual protection/weather context. Generic procedure only; project/supplier details control. |
| bda-mortar-2023 | handbook | Brick Development Association, Mortar for Brickwork, April 2023, 18 pages. https://d23z90m210izbx.cloudfront.net/uploads/downloads/08.-Mortar-for-Brickwork-Technical-Guide-2023.f1678701358.pdf | pp5-6 function and design selection; pp8-10 water/suction, actual mixing and waste; pp12-14 cementitious factory dry/silo states; p15 lime-only alternatives outside this route. No universal numeric conversion or curing factor. |
| ancon-restraint-interface | extension_guidance | Ancon, What is a Head Restraint and why are Head Restraints required in masonry panels?, 8 May 2017. https://www.ancon.co.uk/blog/what-is-a-head-restraint-and-why-are-head-restraints-required-in-masonry-panels | Designer-controlled top restraint and movement interface, compatibility and omission consequences only; no article dimensions/spacing/load factors are imported into this PCR. |
